# Commands · S · part 5

1,475 entries, sdp-fe-site-identity to security. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sdp-fe-site-identity**: Use when you need to match an existing brand from a live site URL or screenshots, redesign while keeping its look, or audit visual consistency across SDP projects. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-site-identity.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-table**: Use when you need to display a collection of records with pagination, sorting and per-row actions in a page. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-table.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-finish**: Use quando implementacao e revisao estiverem concluidas e a branch de feature precisar ser finalizada. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-finish.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-implement**: Use when you have a complete tasks.md plan and an approved plan.md ready for implementation. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-implement.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-mr-checklist**: Use when a GitLab MR exists and test plan checkboxes need to be populated before review. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-mr-checklist.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-mr-template**: sdp-mr-template ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-mr-template.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-plan**: Use when a feature spec is approved and planning artifacts are missing before implementation begins. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-plan.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-qa**: Use when you need to verify that the implementation actually works, when you need an evidence-based quality score before review, or when automated checks have not been run after recent changes ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-qa.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-review**: Use when an implementation phase or task is complete and needs spec compliance and code quality review before merging or closing. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-review.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-security**: Use when you need a security audit of the project, suspect leaked secrets or credentials, want OWASP/STRIDE threat coverage, or need a dependency vulnerability scan before release. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-security.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-specify**: Use when requirements are vague and must become measurable acceptance criteria, or when a feature branch does not exist yet. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-specify.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-sync**: Sync a ticket between ServiceDesk Plus (via MCP) and the local cache ([mbadali25/useful-claude-add-ons](https://github.com/mbadali25/useful-claude-add-ons/blob/HEAD/plugin/crew/commands/sdp-sync.md) · ★3)
- **sdp-sync**: Sincroniza os slash commands /&lt;skill&gt; com o catálogo atual do Hub SDP (skills novas/atualizadas) ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-sync.md))
- **sdp-sync**: Ressincronize os comandos deste projeto com o catálogo mais recente do Hub SDP. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-sync.md))
- **sdp-tasks**: Use when plan.md and spec.md exist but tasks.md has not been generated or is out of sync. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-tasks.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-taskstoissues**: Use when you need to convert pending tasks from tasks.md into GitLab issues. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-taskstoissues.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-tdd**: Use when an implementation task requires test-first development, when code was written before a failing test exists, or when a bug fix needs a reproducing test before the fix. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-tdd.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-writing-skills**: Use when creating or rewriting a SKILL.md, when a skill fails to load because the description summarises workflow, or when an existing skill exceeds token limits or lacks caveman directive. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-writing-skills.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdr-continue**: Continue an existing SDR research project ([JoeSagera/Spec-Driven-Research](https://github.com/JoeSagera/Spec-Driven-Research/blob/HEAD/install/templates/opencode/commands/sdr-continue.md) · ★2)
- **sdr-ff**: Fast-forward an SDR research project through all phases ([JoeSagera/Spec-Driven-Research](https://github.com/JoeSagera/Spec-Driven-Research/blob/HEAD/install/templates/opencode/commands/sdr-ff.md) · ★2)
- **sdr-new**: Start a new SDR research project ([JoeSagera/Spec-Driven-Research](https://github.com/JoeSagera/Spec-Driven-Research/blob/HEAD/install/templates/opencode/commands/sdr-new.md) · ★2)
- **sds**: Generate System Design Specification ([khairulariffinka/codexen](https://github.com/khairulariffinka/codexen/blob/HEAD/core/commands/sds.md) · ★1)
- **sds**: Shutdown Sequence - autonomous end-of-session wrap-up (commit, issues, reflect, handoff, broadcast, exit) ([lucasmccomb/ccgm](https://github.com/lucasmccomb/ccgm/blob/HEAD/modules/session-lifecycle/commands/sds.md))
- **SDS Spec**: Generate Software Design Specification (SDS) based on user prompt or specified file, executing build-context and SDS generation stages. ([NextEvoEco/contract-coding-skill](https://github.com/NextEvoEco/contract-coding-skill/blob/HEAD/commands/sds-spec.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **se-config-answer-ceiling**: config / answer / ceiling: sets answer.ceiling to what you type. The score where the gate names the band rewrite in its log line. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-ceiling.md))
- **se-config-answer-enabled-false**: config / answer / enabled: sets answer.enabled to false. The door holding the owner's prompt first. false takes it out. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-enabled-false.md))
- **se-config-answer-enabled-true**: config / answer / enabled: sets answer.enabled to true. The door holding the owner's prompt first. false takes it out. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-enabled-true.md))

- **se-config-answer-words**: config / answer / words: sets answer.words to what you type. The words an answer holds outside its code and tables. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-words.md))
- **se-config-ask-wanted-full**: config / ask / wanted: sets ask.wanted to full. What the owner wants said at the end of this turn. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-ask-wanted-full.md))

- **se-config-battery-budget**: config / battery / budget: sets battery.budget to what you type. The time the whole check takes at most. Past it, the check prints a warning naming its slowest part and its slowest cases. 0 switches it off. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-battery-budget.md))
- **se-config-battery-runs**: config / battery / runs: sets battery.runs to what you type. The check's runs the stamp keeps at one commit. A retro reads each part's median over them. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-battery-runs.md))
- **se-config-budget-handBack**: config / budget / handBack: sets budget.handBack to what you type. The median time a hand-back takes to land over a group of many tickets, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-handBack.md))
- **se-config-budget-pull**: config / budget / pull: sets budget.pull to what you type. The median time the pull takes to hand out a leaf over a group of many tickets, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-pull.md))
- **se-config-budget-resolver**: config / budget / resolver: sets budget.resolver to what you type. The median time the guidance resolver takes over many notes, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-resolver.md))
- **se-config-budget-stale**: config / budget / stale: sets budget.stale to what you type. The median time the query for stale steps takes to read, hash and compare the notes a hold names, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-stale.md))
- **se-config-cloud-fleetRoutine**: config / cloud / fleetRoutine: sets cloud.fleetRoutine to what you type. The id of the stored routine that checks the fleet, which ./RUNME.sh cloud trigger names. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-cloud-fleetRoutine.md))

([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-migration-lsp-shadow.md))

- **se-config-migration-phase0-false**: config / migration / phase0: sets migration.phase0 to false. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-migration-phase0-false.md))
- **se-config-migration-phase0-true**: config / migration / phase0: sets migration.phase0 to true. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-migration-phase0-true.md))
- **se-config-migration-phase1-false**: config / migration / phase1: sets migration.phase1 to false. - **se-config-plan-everyCalls**: config / plan / everyCalls: sets plan.everyCalls to what you type. The calls between two asks of the engine's three questions. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-plan-everyCalls.md))
- **se-config-plan-grace**: config / plan / grace: sets plan.grace to what you type. The calls that pass after the ask, before every call waits on the plan's answer. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-plan-grace.md))
- **se-config-plan-mostOpen**: config / plan / mostOpen: sets plan.mostOpen to what you type. The todos the plan holds open, past which the third question stays away. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-plan-mostOpen.md))
- **se-config-processes-placements**: config / processes / placements: sets processes.placements to what you type. the lists of module instances that share one process, where each instance in no list takes a process of its own ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-processes-placements.md))
- **se-config-pull-cap**: config / pull / cap: sets pull.cap to what you type. The size up to which one tool answer reaches the model whole. Past it, the client hands back a preview. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-pull-cap.md))
- **se-config-pull-margin**: config / pull / margin: sets pull.margin to what you type. The room the pull keeps free below the cap. - **se-config-work-personSigns-false**: config / work / personSigns: sets work.personSigns to false. The stronger door on a person's hand. - **se-config-work-progressAfter**: config / work / progressAfter: sets work.progressAfter to what you type. The age of a live hold's tip past which it reads stalled. - **search-contradictions**: - **Assertion（知識の項目）:** ユーザーが知っている可能性を扱う、一つの正規化済みの命題。 - **Concept（話題の見出し）:** Assertion を探しやすくする話題名。例: `Go`、`defer`。 - **Relation（関連）:** Assertion や Concept の間に、明示的に保存するつながり。`contradicts` は「矛盾候補として登録された関連」を表すだけで、正誤を確定しない。 - **検索起点（`seed`）:** この結果で selector に一致した Assertion。Concept selector のときは、その Concept に属する Assertion の一つである。 - **相手（`target`）:** `seed` と同じ Relation の反対側の Assertion。常にこの値を `get` または ([yukihito-jokyu/knowledge](https://github.com/yukihito-jokyu/knowledge/blob/HEAD/documents/docs/features/FEAT-001/design/commands/search-contradictions.md))

- **search-related**: - **Assertion（知識の項目）:** ユーザーが知っている可能性を扱う、一つの正規化済みの命題。 - **Concept（話題の見出し）:** Assertion を探しやすくする話題名。例: `Go`、`defer`。 - **Relation（関連）:** Assertion や Concept の間に、明示的に保存するつながり。システムが意味から自動推測するものではない。 - **検索起点:** 関連を探し始める Assertion または Concept。結果の `target` は、この検索起点の相手を表す。 ([yukihito-jokyu/knowledge](https://github.com/yukihito-jokyu/knowledge/blob/HEAD/documents/docs/features/FEAT-001/design/commands/search-related.md))

- **search-temporal**: - **Assertion（知識の項目）:** ユーザーが知っている可能性を扱う、一つの正規化済みの命題。 - **Concept（話題の見出し）:** Assertion を探しやすくする話題名。例: `Go`、`defer`。 - **Scope（適用条件）:** その Assertion が成り立つ範囲。例: 対象バージョンや実行環境。 - **時点情報:** 有効期間、観測時刻、最終確認時刻など、Assertion がいつの情報かを示す記録。 ([yukihito-jokyu/knowledge](https://github.com/yukihito-jokyu/knowledge/blob/HEAD/documents/docs/features/FEAT-001/design/commands/search-temporal.md))

- **search-text**: - **Assertion（知識の項目）:** ユーザーが知っている可能性を扱う、一つの正規化済みの命題。例:「Go の `defer` は関数を抜けるときに実行される」。 - **Concept（話題の見出し）:** Assertion を探しやすくする話題名。例: `Go`、`defer`。 - **Alias（別名）:** API 名や識別子など、同じ Assertion を探すための別の検索語。 - **Scope（適用条件）:** その Assertion が成り立つ範囲。例: 対象バージョンや実行環境。 - **字句照合:** 単語や文字列が一致するかで探す方法。言い換えた同じ意味を探す意味検索は、初期提供には含まれない。 ([yukihito-jokyu/knowledge](https://github.com/yukihito-jokyu/knowledge/blob/HEAD/documents/docs/features/FEAT-001/design/commands/search-text.md))

- **section.es**: Cómo SECCION (SECTION, alias SC) de AutoCAD en español crea la región donde un plano corta sólidos 3D: 3puntos, Objeto, ejeZ, Vista, XY, YZ o ZX. Pruébalo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/section.es.md))
